; ============================================================================
; Amstrad CPC464 - Locomotive BASIC 1.0 upper ROM (16 KB), loaded at &C000
; Disassembled with z80dasm + custom code/data tracing; reassembles with pasmo
; to a byte-identical copy of the source ROM.
;
; Notes
;  - Entry point &C006. ROM header at &C000, name table at &C04C.
;  - "call expect_char_inline" (&DD37) is followed by ONE inline byte: the
;    character the BASIC text must contain next (e.g. '(' ')' ',').
;  - raise_error (&CA94): error number in E; messages at err_msg_00..30.
;  - stmt_dispatch_table: entry n handles token &80+n (verified vs keyword table).
;  - Key tables (&CFF2, &EC2C, &F224) are searched by &FF93:
;    count, then (handler addr, key byte) entries, then default addr.
;  - fn_dispatch_table token mapping NOT verified.
;  - Names raise_error / expect_char_inline are inferred from behaviour.
;  - Code vs data in a few small areas was decided heuristically.
; ============================================================================
KM_WAIT_CHAR:	equ 0xbb06
KM_READ_CHAR:	equ 0xbb09
KM_CHAR_RETURN:	equ 0xbb0c
KM_SET_EXPAND:	equ 0xbb0f
KM_TEST_KEY:	equ 0xbb1e
KM_GET_JOYSTICK:	equ 0xbb24
KM_SET_TRANSLATE:	equ 0xbb27
KM_SET_SHIFT:	equ 0xbb2d
KM_SET_CONTROL:	equ 0xbb33
KM_SET_REPEAT:	equ 0xbb39
KM_SET_DELAY:	equ 0xbb3f
KM_ARM_BREAK:	equ 0xbb45
KM_DISARM_BREAK:	equ 0xbb48
TXT_VDU_ENABLE:	equ 0xbb54

GRA_TEST_RELATIVE:	equ 0xbbf3
GRA_LINE_ABSOLUTE:	equ 0xbbf6
GRA_LINE_RELATIVE:	equ 0xbbf9
SCR_SET_MODE:	equ 0xbc0e
SCR_SET_INK:	equ 0xbc32
SCR_SET_BORDER:	equ 0xbc38
SCR_SET_FLASHING:	equ 0xbc3e
CAS_SET_SPEED:	equ 0xbc68
CAS_NOISY:	equ 0xbc6b
CAS_IN_OPEN:	equ 0xbc77
CAS_IN_CLOSE:	equ 0xbc7a
CAS_IN_ABANDON:	equ 0xbc7d
CAS_IN_CHAR:	equ 0xbc80
CAS_IN_DIRECT:	equ 0xbc83
CAS_RETURN:	equ 0xbc86
CAS_TEST_EOF:	equ 0xbc89
CAS_OUT_OPEN:	equ 0xbc8c
CAS_OUT_CLOSE:	equ 0xbc8f
CAS_OUT_ABANDON:	equ 0xbc92
CAS_OUT_CHAR:	equ 0xbc95
CAS_OUT_DIRECT:	equ 0xbc98
CAS_CATALOG:	equ 0xbc9b
SOUND_RESET:	equ 0xbca7
SOUND_QUEUE:	equ 0xbcaa
SOUND_CHECK:	equ 0xbcad
SOUND_ARM_EVENT:	equ 0xbcb0
SOUND_RELEASE:	equ 0xbcb3
SOUND_HOLD:	equ 0xbcb6
SOUND_CONTINUE:	equ 0xbcb9
SOUND_AMPL_ENVELOPE:	equ 0xbcbc
SOUND_TONE_ENVELOPE:	equ 0xbcbf
KL_ROM_WALK:	equ 0xbccb
KL_FIND_COMMAND:	equ 0xbcd4
KL_ADD_TICKER:	equ 0xbce9
KL_DEL_TICKER:	equ 0xbcec
KL_INIT_EVENT:	equ 0xbcef
KL_SYNC_RESET:	equ 0xbcf5
KL_NEXT_SYNC:	equ 0xbcfb
KL_DO_SYNC:	equ 0xbcfe

KL_EVENT_DISABLE:	equ 0xbd04

	org 0c000h

kw_letter_table:
; ---- keyword list pointers: entry n -> list of keywords starting with letter A+n ----
	defw kw_list_A		; e354  'A'
	defw kw_list_B		; e356  'B'
	defw kw_list_C		; e358  'C'
	defw kw_list_D		; e35a  'D'
	defw kw_list_E		; e35c  'E'
	defw kw_list_F		; e35e  'F'
	defw kw_list_G		; e360  'G'
	defw kw_list_H		; e362  'H'
	defw kw_list_I		; e364  'I'
	defw kw_list_J		; e366  'J'
	defw kw_list_K		; e368  'K'
	defw kw_list_L		; e36a  'L'
	defw kw_list_M		; e36c  'M'
	defw kw_list_N		; e36e  'N'
	defw kw_list_O		; e370  'O'
	defw kw_list_P		; e372  'P'
	defw kw_list_Q		; e374  'Q'
	defw kw_list_R		; e376  'R'
	defw kw_list_S		; e378  'S'
	defw kw_list_T		; e37a  'T'
	defw kw_list_U		; e37c  'U'
	defw kw_list_V		; e37e  'V'
	defw kw_list_W		; e380  'W'
	defw kw_list_X		; e382  'X'
	defw kw_list_Y		; e384  'Y'
	defw kw_list_Z		; e386  'Z'

; ---- BASIC keyword table ----
keyword_lists:
; Lists are in reverse letter order (Z first, A last). Each entry = keyword text WITHOUT its first
; letter, last char has bit 7 set, followed by the token byte; a 00 byte ends each letter list.
kw_list_Z:
	defb "ON",0c5h,0dah		; e388  ZONE -> token &DA
